=== runner.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Runner скоростного контура (A1/A2/A4/A5) — бесконечный цикл для VPS.

Расписание (deploy/config.yaml, МСК):
- Одинцово: база 3 мин (диапазон 2–5), 07:00–23:00;
- Москва: 15 мин; прочие города МО: 17 мин;
- ночью (23:00–07:00): полный прогон всех городов раз в час;
- джиттер ±30 с на каждом интервале (город выходит на ход не ровно по часам).

Сбор: Яндекс (collect_cities) + ЦИАН (collect_city_html) → data/listings.json
→ json_to_db.py → конвертер заполняет detected_at/published_at (A4).
Новые частники попадают в notify_queue (db_schema): ночью копятся до утра
(A5), днём немедленную отправку включим в Части D.

Надёжность (H1-H3): ошибка одного города не роняет цикл, каждый выход
в сеть под таймаутами и повторами (сам parser_*), зависший запрос не
подвешивает цикл; БД пушится в git с pull --rebase перед push.
"""
import json
import os
import random
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time as time_mod
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import yaml

import db_schema
import metrics

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
MSK = timezone(timedelta(hours=3))
CONFIG_PATH = os.environ.get("REALTOR_CONFIG",
                             os.path.join(ROOT, "deploy", "config.yaml"))
DB_PATH = "listings.db"


def log(msg):
    print("[%s] %s" % (datetime.now(MSK).strftime("%d.%m %H:%M:%S"), msg),
          flush=True)


def load_config(path=CONFIG_PATH):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return yaml.safe_load(f)


def msk_now():
    return datetime.now(MSK)


def is_night(now):
    """Ночная тишина (A5): 23:00–07:00 уведомления копятся в очереди."""
    return now.hour >= 23 or now.hour < 7


def city_due(city, last_ts, now_ts, interval_minutes, jitter=30.0):
    """True, если до города дошёл ход: интервал ± джиттер; впервой раз — сразу."""
    if last_ts is None:
        return True
    target = interval_minutes * 60.0
    return (now_ts - last_ts) >= target + random.uniform(-jitter, jitter)


def pick_due(cities, last_runs, now_ts, interval_minutes, jitter=30.0):
    return [c for c in cities
            if city_due(c, last_runs.get(c), now_ts, interval_minutes, jitter)]


def cycle_plan(cfg, now, last_runs=None, jitter=30.0):
    """План цикла. Ночью — полный прогон (interval из cfg[night]),
    днём — каждая зона со своим интервалом и своими «на ход» городами."""
    last_runs = last_runs or {}
    hot_cfg = cfg["hot"]
    reg_cfg = cfg["regular"]
    moscow_cfg = cfg.get("moscow") or {"cities": [],
                                       "interval_minutes": reg_cfg["interval_minutes"]}
    now_ts = now.timestamp()
    if is_night(now):
        all_cities = list(dict.fromkeys(
            hot_cfg["cities"] + moscow_cfg["cities"] + reg_cfg["cities"]))
        return {"kind": "night", "all": all_cities,
                "interval": cfg["night"]["every_minutes"] * 60.0}
    return {
        "kind": "day",
        "hot": pick_due(hot_cfg["cities"], last_runs, now_ts,
                        hot_cfg["interval_minutes"], jitter),
        "regular": pick_due(reg_cfg["cities"], last_runs, now_ts,
                            reg_cfg["interval_minutes"], jitter),
        "moscow": pick_due(moscow_cfg["cities"], last_runs, now_ts,
                           moscow_cfg["interval_minutes"], jitter),
        "intervals": {"hot": hot_cfg["interval_minutes"] * 60.0,
                      "regular": reg_cfg["interval_minutes"] * 60.0,
                      "moscow": moscow_cfg["interval_minutes"] * 60.0},
    }


def run_convert(root):
    """Конвертер JSON → БД (заполняет detected_at/published_at у новых)."""
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, os.path.join(root, "json_to_db.py")], cwd=root,
        capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
        timeout=900, env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONUTF8="1"))
    ok = proc.returncode == 0
    if not ok:
        log("[ОШИБКА] json_to_db: код %s: %s" %
            (proc.returncode, ((proc.stderr or "") + (proc.stdout or ""))[-300:]))
    return ok


def category_of(rooms):
    try:
        n = int(str(rooms or ""))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        n = 0
    if n in (1, 2, 3):
        return "%s-комн" % n
    return "другое"


def queue_new_owners(db_path=DB_PATH, since_iso=None, period_kind="day"):
    """Новые частники за цикл → notify_queue (A5). Возвращает список."""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    try:
        db_schema.ensure(conn)
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT url, city, rooms FROM listings "
            "WHERE is_owner=1 AND price IS NOT NULL AND detected_at >= ?",
            (since_iso,)).fetchall()
        now_iso = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        chat_id = os.getenv("REALTOR_CHAT_ID", "")
        for url, city, rooms in rows:
            db_schema.queue_notice(conn, url, category_of(rooms),
                                   chat_id, now_iso)
        if rows:
            log("[ОЧЕРЕДЬ] новых частников: %d (%s)" %
                (len(rows), "ночь — копим до утра" if period_kind == "night"
                 else "день — к отправке"))
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return rows


def git_push_db(root, interval_minutes=15, last_push=None):
    """Пушит listings.db в git не чаще interval; перед push — pull --rebase.
    Токен — из env (deploy/env.example). Отвалившийся push не роняет цикл."""
    now_ts = time_mod.time()
    if last_push is not None and now_ts - last_push < interval_minutes * 60:
        return last_push
    token = os.getenv("GITHUB_REPO_TOKEN")
    if not token:
        return last_push or now_ts
    remote = "https://x-access-token:%s@github.com/isaafoks-collab/realtor-service.git" % token
    env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONUTF8="1", GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT="0")

    def git(*args):
        proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=root, env=env,
                              capture_output=True, text=True,
                              encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=180)
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            log("[GIT] %s: не удалось (%s)" %
                (args[0], ((proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or ""))[-200:]))
        return proc.returncode == 0

    try:
        if not git("add", DB_PATH):
            return now_ts
        dirty_proc = subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "--cached", "--quiet"],
                                    cwd=root, env=env, capture_output=True)
        if dirty_proc.returncode == 0:
            return now_ts
        if git("-c", "user.name=realtor-runner",
               "-c", "user.email=runner@realtor.service",
               "commit", "-m", "runner: база объявлений"):
            git("pull", "--rebase", remote, "main")
            git("push", remote, "main")
            log("[GIT] база обновлена в git")
    except Exception as e:
        log("[GIT] сбой: %s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e))
    return now_ts


def collect(cities, data, root, seen):
    """Сбор по городам: Яндекс + ЦИАН-HTML. Ошибка города не роняет цикл."""
    import parser_cian
    import parser_yandex
    from httpclient import session as make_session

    # Яндекс: уже сам проходит города с ретраями и паузами
    parser_yandex.collect_cities(cities, data)

    sess = make_session()
    sess.headers.update({"User-Agent": parser_cian.UA,
                         "Accept-Language": "ru-RU,ru;q=0.9"})
    owner_map = {str(lid): "" for lid in data["owners"]}
    listings = data["listings"]
    owners_dict = data.setdefault("_owners_io", owner_map)
    for city in cities:
        try:
            parser_cian.collect_city_html(sess, city, listings, owners_dict, seen)
            parser_yandex.pause(6)
        except Exception as e:
            log("[ОШИБКА] ЦИАН %s: %s: %s" % (city, type(e).__name__, e))
    data.pop("_owners_io", None)
    data["owners"] = list(owners_dict)


def run_cycle(cfg, root):
    """Один проход: сбор по расписанию → JSON → конвертация → очередь."""
    data_path = os.path.join(root, "data", "listings.json")
    data = {"listings": {}, "owners": []}
    if os.path.exists(data_path):
        raw = json.load(open(data_path, encoding="utf-8"))
        if "listings" in raw:
            data = {"listings": raw["listings"], "owners": raw.get("owners", [])}
    seen = set(data["listings"])
    start_iso = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

    plan = cycle_plan(cfg, msk_now(), runner_get_last(root))
    if plan["kind"] == "night":
        cities = plan["all"]
        if not cities:
            return False
        log("[НОЧЬ] полный прогон: %d городов" % len(cities))
    else:
        cities = plan["hot"] + plan["regular"] + plan["moscow"]
        if not cities:
            return False
        log("[ДЕНЬ] на ход: %s" % ", ".join(cities))
        if "{hot}" in plan.get("intervals") or False:
            pass  # страховка от забытых хвостов — не должна появляться

    collect(cities, data, root, seen)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(data_path), exist_ok=True)
    json.dump({"listings": data["listings"], "owners": data["owners"]},
              open(data_path, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False)
    if run_convert(root):
        queue_new_owners(DB_PATH, since_iso=start_iso, period_kind=plan["kind"])

    now_ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
    new_last = dict(runner_get_last(root))
    for c in cities:
        new_last[c] = now_ts
    runner_set_last(root, new_last)
    return True


_STATE_CACHE = {}


def _state_path(root):
    return os.environ.get("REALTOR_RUNNER_STATE",
                          os.path.join(root, "data", "runner_state.json"))


def runner_get_last(root):
    path = _state_path(root)
    if path in _STATE_CACHE:
        return dict(_STATE_CACHE[path])
    if os.path.exists(path):
        try:
            _STATE_CACHE[path] = json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            _STATE_CACHE[path] = {}
    else:
        _STATE_CACHE[path] = {}
    return dict(_STATE_CACHE[path])


def runner_set_last(root, state):
    path = _state_path(root)
    _STATE_CACHE[path] = dict(state)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    json.dump(state, open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False,
              indent=1)


def summary_metrics():
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
        text = metrics.report_text(conn)
        conn.close()
        print(text)
    except Exception as e:
        log("[ОШИБКА] метрики: %s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e))


def main():
    cfg = load_config()
    log("Runner стартует; конфиг: %s" % CONFIG_PATH)
    tick = int(cfg.get("tick_seconds", 30))
    push_every = int((cfg.get("push") or {}).get("every_minutes", 15))
    last_push = None
    while True:
        try:
            run_cycle(cfg, ROOT)
        except Exception as e:  # H2: любое падение — с контекстом в лог
            log("[ОШИБКА] цикл: %s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, e))
        last_push = git_push_db(ROOT, push_every, last_push)
        time_mod.sleep(tick)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "метрики":
        summary_metrics()
    else:
        main()

=== scheduler.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
﻿# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Планировщик: раз в N часов — ЦИАН, Яндекс, конвертер, анти-фильтр, отчёт."""
import subprocess, sys, time, random
from datetime import datetime

INTERVAL_HOURS = 6

def run(script):
    print("[%s] >> %s" % (datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S"), script))
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, script], timeout=7200,
                           capture_output=True, text=True, errors="replace")
        tail = (r.stdout or "").strip().splitlines()
        for line in tail[-3:]:
            print("   ", line)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            err = (r.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()
            print("   [ERR]", err[-1] if err else "код %d" % r.returncode)
            return False
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("   [ERR]", type(e).__name__, e)
        return False

def report():
    try:
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect("listings.db")
        total = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM listings").fetchone()[0]
        owners = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM listings WHERE is_owner=1").fetchone()[0]
        fresh = conn.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM listings WHERE last_seen >= datetime('now', '-1 day')"
        ).fetchone()[0]
        conn.close()
        line = "[%s] база: всего=%d, частников=%d, видны за сутки=%d" % (
            datetime.now().strftime("%d.%m %H:%M"), total, owners, fresh)
        print(line)
        with open("scheduler.log", "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(line + "\n")
    except Exception as e:
        print("[ERR] отчёт:", e)

print("планировщик запущен, цикл каждые %d ч. Ctrl+C — стоп." % INTERVAL_HOURS)
while True:
    print("=" * 50)
    for script in ("parser_cian.py", "parser_yandex.py", "json_to_db.py"):
        run(script)
        time.sleep(random.uniform(20, 60))
    report()
    time.sleep(INTERVAL_HOURS * 3600)


=== setup.ps1 ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
﻿# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
param(
    [string]$TokenFile,
    [switch]$SkipGitHub
)
# Скрипт начальной настройки: BOT_TOKEN / BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS.
# Источники по порядку: 1) окружение 2) токены.txt 3) секреты проекта
# (GitHub Secrets через gh: наличие через gh secret list; update через
# gh secret set) 4) ручной ввод (скрытый, без эха).
# Значения НИКОГДА не печатаются ни в окно, ни в лог, ни в чат.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
if (-not $TokenFile) { $TokenFile = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'токены.txt' }
$names = @('BOT_TOKEN', 'BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS')

function Read-TokenFile($path) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { return @{} }
    $map = @{}
    foreach ($line in (Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Encoding UTF8)) {
        if ($line -match '^\s*#' -or $line -notmatch '=') { continue }
        $eq = $line.IndexOf('=')
        $key = $line.Substring(0, $eq).Trim()
        $val = $line.Substring($eq + 1).Trim()
        if ($key) { $map[$key] = $val }
    }
    return $map
}

function Read-Secret($name) {
    Write-Host "Введите $name (ввод скрыт, пустая строка — пропустить): " -NoNewline
    if ([Console]::IsInputRedirected) {
        $raw = [Console]::In.ReadLine()
    } else {
        $sec = Read-Host '' -AsSecureString
        $bstr = [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::SecureStringToBSTR($sec)
        try {
            $raw = [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::PtrToStringUni($bstr)
        } finally {
            [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ZeroFreeBSTR($bstr)
        }
    }
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($raw)) { return $null }
    return $raw.Trim()
}

function Merge-TokenFile($path, $name, $value) {
    $lines = @()
    $utf8 = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
    if (Test-Path $path) {
        $lines = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllLines($path, $utf8)
    }
    $found = $false
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $lines.Count; $i++) {
        $eq = $lines[$i].IndexOf('=')
        if ($eq -gt 0 -and $lines[$i].Substring(0, $eq).Trim() -eq $name) {
            $lines[$i] = "$name=$value"
            $found = $true
        }
    }
    if (-not $found) { $lines += "$name=$value" }
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllLines($path, $lines, $utf8)
}

# Синхронизация секретов в GitHub (значения не выводятся, только имена).
function Sync-GitHubSecret($name, $value) {
    if ($SkipGitHub) { return $false }
    if (-not (Get-Command gh -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) { return $false }
    $null = & gh secret set $name --body $value 2>&1
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
        Write-Host "${name}: секрет GitHub обновлён (значение не напечатано)"
        return $true
    }
    Write-Host "${name}: не удалось обновить GitHub Secrets (gh auth проверьте)"
    return $false
}

$fromGitHub = @{}
if (-not $SkipGitHub -and (Get-Command gh -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
    $list = (& gh secret list --json name 2>$null | ConvertFrom-Json) -as [array]
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and $list) {
        $ghNames = @($list | ForEach-Object { $_.name })
        foreach ($n in $names) {
            if ($ghNames -contains $n) { $fromGitHub[$n] = $true }
        }
    }
}

$values = @{}
$sources = @{}
foreach ($name in $names) {
    $value = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable($name)
    $source = 'окружение'
    if ($value) {
        $values[$name] = $value
    } else {
        $tokenMap = Read-TokenFile $TokenFile
        if ($tokenMap.ContainsKey($name) -and $tokenMap[$name]) {
            $value = $tokenMap[$name]
            $source = 'токены.txt'
            $values[$name] = $value
        } else {
            $value = Read-Secret $name
            if ($value) {
                $source = 'ручной ввод'
                $values[$name] = $value
                if (-not ($tokenMap.ContainsKey($name) -and $tokenMap[$name])) {
                    Merge-TokenFile $TokenFile $name $value
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if ($value) { $sources[$name] = $source }
    if ($value) {
        $null = Sync-GitHubSecret $name $value
    }
}

Write-Host ''
Write-Host '=== Сводка ==='
foreach ($name in $names) {
    $local = if ($sources.ContainsKey($name)) { $true } else { $false }
    if ($local) {
        Write-Host "${name}: заполнен (источник: $($sources[$name]))" -ForegroundColor Green
    } else {
        Write-Host "${name}: не задан"
        if ($fromGitHub.ContainsKey($name)) {
            Write-Host "${name}: есть в GitHub Secrets (значение недоступно для чтения)," -ForegroundColor Yellow
            Write-Host '  workflow на GitHub получит его автоматически в Actions.'
        } else {
            Write-Host "${name}: нет ни локально, ни в GitHub Secrets" -ForegroundColor Red
        }
    }
}
Write-Host ''
Write-Host 'Значения не выводились (ни в окно, ни в лог).'
Write-Host "Локальный файл токенов: $TokenFile"

﻿@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
title Praymo bots launcher
echo === ZAPUSK DVOCH BOTOV PRAYMO ===
rem Tokeny berutsya iz okruzheniya (BOT_TOKEN, BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS),
rem esli ne zadany — iz lokalnogo faila tokenty.txt (v git ne vhodit).
rem Znacheniya ni v okno, ni v log ne vyvodyatsya.
if not defined BOT_TOKEN if exist "%~dp0токены.txt" (
    for /f "usebackq tokens=1,* delims== eol=#" %%a in ("%~dp0токены.txt") do set "%%a=%%b"
)
if not defined BOT_TOKEN (
    echo OSIBKA: ne zadan BOT_TOKEN.
    echo Zadayte peremennuyu okruzheniya: setx BOT_TOKEN "..." i otkroite okno zanovo,
    echo libo pomestite BOT_TOKEN=... v fail tokenty.txt ryadom s start.bat.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
start "PRAYMO - podborka sobstvennikov" cmd /k "cd /d "%~dp0" && python bot.py"
timeout /t 2 >nul
if not defined BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS (
    echo OSIBKA: ne zadan BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS.
    echo Zadayte setx BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS "..." libo vnesite v tokenty.txt.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
set "BOT_TOKEN=%BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS%"
set "BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS="
start "PRAYMO - dlya sobstvennikov" cmd /k "cd /d "%~dp0" && python bot.py"
echo.
echo Oba bota zapushcheny v otdelnyh oknah.
echo Okna mozhno svernut - boty prodolzhat rabotu.
timeout /t 5 >nul

=== test_access.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
"""14 тестов модуля вечного доступа. Запуск: python test_access.py"""
import os, tempfile, unittest
import access

class TestAccess(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.old_db = access.DB_PATH
        access.DB_PATH = os.path.join(self.tmpdir, "t.db")
        os.environ["ACCESS_LOG"] = os.path.join(self.tmpdir, "log.txt")

    def tearDown(self):
        access.DB_PATH = self.old_db

    def test_01_wrong_code_denied(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(1, "NOPE", "PRYMO-2026"), "deny")

    def test_02_correct_code_ok(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(2, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026"), "ok")

    def test_03_second_grant_again(self):
        access.grant(3, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026")
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(3, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026"), "again")

    def test_04_has_access_true(self):
        access.grant(4, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026")
        self.assertTrue(access.has_access(4))

    def test_05_has_access_false(self):
        self.assertFalse(access.has_access(5))

    def test_06_case_insensitive(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(6, "prymo-2026", "PRYMO-2026"), "ok")

    def test_07_spaces_trimmed(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(7, " PRYMO-2026 ", "PRYMO-2026"), "ok")

    def test_08_empty_code_denied(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(8, "", "PRYMO-2026"), "deny")

    def test_09_log_written(self):
        access.grant(9, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026")
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.environ["ACCESS_LOG"]))

    def test_10_deny_logged(self):
        access.grant(10, "XXX", "PRYMO-2026")
        with open(os.environ["ACCESS_LOG"], encoding="utf-8") as f:
            self.assertIn("DENY user=10", f.read())

    def test_11_persist_after_reopen(self):
        access.grant(11, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026")
        self.assertTrue(access.has_access(11))

    def test_12_users_isolated(self):
        access.grant(12, "PRYMO-2026", "PRYMO-2026")
        self.assertFalse(access.has_access(13))

    def test_13_no_crash_on_repeat_deny(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(1, "NOPE", "PRYMO-2026"), "deny")

    def test_14_custom_code_in_env(self):
        self.assertEqual(access.grant(14, "ZZ-0001", "ZZ-0001"), "ok")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_db_backup.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""db_backup: WAL-безопасный бэкап и restore-проверка.
Запуск: python -m unittest test_db_backup"""
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import db_backup


def make_src(path):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    conn.execute("CREATE TABLE listings "
                 "(rowid_ INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, city TEXT, price REAL)")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO listings (city, price) VALUES (?,?)",
                     [("Одинцово", 7_200_000), ("Саратов", 3_500_000)])
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


class DbBackupTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.src = os.path.join(self.tmp.name, "listings.db")
        self.dst = os.path.join(self.tmp.name, "backup.sqlite")
        make_src(self.src)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def test_backup_via_backup_api_preserves_last_row(self):
        db_backup.backup(self.src, self.dst)
        self.assertTrue(db_backup.verify(self.src, self.dst))

    def test_verify_detects_corrupted_backup(self):
        db_backup.backup(self.src, self.dst)
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dst)
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM listings WHERE rowid=(SELECT MAX(rowid) FROM listings)")
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        self.assertFalse(db_backup.verify(self.src, self.dst))

    def test_verify_detects_missing_table(self):
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.dst)
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE other (x)")
        conn.close()
        self.assertFalse(db_backup.verify(self.src, self.dst))

    def test_cli_exit_codes(self):
        self.assertEqual(db_backup.main(["db_backup.py", self.src, self.dst]), 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_httpclient.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Приёмы Miraines в httpclient: pause() и fetch_with_backoff().
Запуск: python -m unittest test_httpclient"""
import os
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import httpclient


def fake_send(seq):
    """seq: список (response|Exception) — выдаётся по одному за вызов."""
    state = {"i": 0}

    def sender(url, **kw):
        item = seq[state["i"]]
        state["i"] += 1
        if isinstance(item, Exception):
            raise item
        return item

    sender.count = lambda: state["i"]
    return sender


class Resp:
    def __init__(self, code=200):
        self.status_code = code


class TestPause(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_pause_within_jitter_bounds(self):
        sleeps = []
        httpclient.pause(10, jitter=0.3, sleep=sleeps.append)
        for _ in range(50):
            httpclient.pause(10, jitter=0.3, sleep=sleeps.append)
        self.assertTrue(all(7.0 <= s <= 13.0 for s in sleeps), sleeps[:5])


class TestFetchWithBackoff(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_retries_429_then_200(self):
        sleeps = []
        send = fake_send([Resp(429), Resp(429), Resp(200)])
        r = httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x",
                                          sleep=sleeps.append, fallback_sleep=2.0)
        self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(sleeps, [2.0, 4.0])

    def test_ok_immediate(self):
        sleeps = []
        send = fake_send([Resp(200)])
        httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x", sleep=sleeps.append)
        self.assertEqual(sleeps, [])

    def test_retries_on_439_and_403(self):
        for code in (439, 403):
            sleeps = []
            send = fake_send([Resp(code), Resp(200)])
            r = httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x", sleep=sleeps.append)
            self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 200)
            self.assertEqual(sleeps, [2.0])

    def test_network_error_retried_then_success(self):
        sleeps = []
        send = fake_send([ConnectionError("битый сокет"), Resp(200)])
        r = httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x", sleep=sleeps.append)
        self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(send.count(), 2)

    def test_exhausted_all_429_returns_last(self):
        send = fake_send([Resp(429), Resp(429), Resp(429)])
        r = httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x",
                                          sleep=lambda d: None, attempts=3)
        self.assertEqual(r.status_code, 429)

    def test_network_error_last_attempt_raises(self):
        send = fake_send([ConnectionError("x"), ConnectionError("y")])
        with self.assertRaises(ConnectionError):
            httpclient.fetch_with_backoff(send, "http://x",
                                          sleep=lambda d: None, attempts=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_notify.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""notify: тротлер+ретраи. Запуск: python -m unittest test_notify"""
import sys
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, ".")
import notify


def make_notifier():
    class FakeClock:
        t = 0.0

        def monotonic(self):
            return self.t

        def sleep(self, d):
            self.t += d
            self.sleeps.append(d)

    FakeClock.sleeps = []
    return FakeClock()


SENT = []


def fake_sender(chat_id, text, **kw):
    SENT.append((chat_id, text))
    return "ok"


class TestNotifier(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_private_chat_min_one_sec(self):
        c = make_notifier()
        n = notify.Notifier(clock=c.monotonic, sleep=c.sleep)
        s = n.wrap(fake_sender)
        skept = s(100, "привет")
        s(100, "ещё")  # сразу же — обязан уснуть ~1 сек
        self.assertEqual(skept, "ok")
        self.assertGreaterEqual([x for x in c.sleeps if x > 0][0], 0.9)

    def test_group_chat_throttled_harder(self):
        c = make_notifier()
        n = notify.Notifier(clock=c.monotonic, sleep=c.sleep)
        s = n.wrap(fake_sender)
        s(-500, "в группу")
        c.sleeps.clear()
        s(-500, "в группу второй")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(max(c.sleeps), notify.GROUP_INTERVAL - 0.5)

    def test_global_limit_30_per_second(self):
        c = make_notifier()
        n = notify.Notifier(clock=c.monotonic, sleep=c.sleep)
        s = n.wrap(fake_sender)
        for i in range(31):
            s(1000 + i, "x")
        # 31-я отправка за 1 сек: глобальный лимит принудил паузу
        self.assertGreater(max(c.sleeps), 0)

    def test_retry_three_attempts_then_success(self):
        c = make_notifier()
        n = notify.Notifier(clock=c.monotonic, sleep=c.sleep)
        calls = []

        def flaky(chat_id, text, **kw):
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) < 3:
                raise TimeoutError("сеть мигнула")
            return "ok"

        self.assertEqual(n.call(flaky, 5, "текст"), "ok")
        self.assertEqual(len(calls), 3)

    def test_retry_raises_after_attempts_exhausted(self):
        c = make_notifier()
        n = notify.Notifier(clock=c.monotonic, sleep=c.sleep)

        def always_fail(chat_id, text, **kw):
            raise TimeoutError("бессрочный сбой")

        with self.assertRaises(Exception):
            n.call(always_fail, 5, "текст")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_orchestrator.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Тесты оркестратора. Запуск: python -m unittest test_orchestrator"""
import io
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from contextlib import redirect_stdout

import orchestrator


def run_raw(func, *args, **kwargs):
    """Запускает func, возвращает (результат, stdout)."""
    buf = io.StringIO()
    with redirect_stdout(buf):
        result = func(*args, **kwargs)
    return result, buf.getvalue()


class TestMain(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_no_args_returns_1(self):
        rc, out = run_raw(orchestrator.main, [])
        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)

    def test_unknown_mode_returns_1(self):
        rc, out = run_raw(orchestrator.main, ["bogus"])
        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)

    def test_no_args_prints_available_modes(self):
        rc, out = run_raw(orchestrator.main, [])
        for mode in orchestrator.MODES:
            self.assertIn(mode, out)

    def test_unknown_mode_prints_available_modes(self):
        rc, out = run_raw(orchestrator.main, ["bogus"])
        for mode in orchestrator.MODES:
            self.assertIn(mode, out)


class TestEnsureRequirements(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_creates_minimal_file_when_missing(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            created = orchestrator.ensure_requirements(root)
            path = os.path.join(root, "requirements.txt")
            self.assertTrue(created)
            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(path))
            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                content = f.read()
            for pkg in ("pyTelegramBotAPI", "requests", "beautifulsoup4", "PyYAML"):
                self.assertIn(pkg, content)

    def test_keeps_existing_file_untouched(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            path = os.path.join(root, "requirements.txt")
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("myownpkg==1.0.0\n")
            created = orchestrator.ensure_requirements(root)
            self.assertFalse(created)
            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                self.assertEqual(f.read(), "myownpkg==1.0.0\n")


class TestRunScript(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.root = tempfile.mkdtemp()

    def test_captures_success(self):
        script = os.path.join(self.root, "ok.py")
        with open(script, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write('print("Всё хорошо")\n')
        step, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_script, "ok.py", self.root)
        self.assertFalse(step["skipped"])
        self.assertEqual(step["returncode"], 0)
        self.assertTrue(step["ok"])
        self.assertIn("Всё хорошо", step["output"])

    def test_reports_failure(self):
        script = os.path.join(self.root, "fail.py")
        with open(script, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("import sys; sys.exit(3)\n")
        step, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_script, "fail.py", self.root)
        self.assertEqual(step["returncode"], 3)
        self.assertFalse(step["ok"])

    def test_reports_timeout(self):
        script = os.path.join(self.root, "slow.py")
        with open(script, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("import time; time.sleep(5)\n")
        step, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_script, "slow.py", self.root, timeout=1)
        self.assertTrue(step["timed_out"])
        self.assertFalse(step["ok"])

    def test_missing_script_is_skipped_with_reason(self):
        step, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_script, "nope.py", self.root)
        self.assertTrue(step["skipped"])
        self.assertIn("не найден", step["reason"])


class TestCollectTestResults(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_counts_passed_and_failed(self):
        root = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        with open(os.path.join(root, "test_good.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(
                "import unittest\n"
                "class TestGood(unittest.TestCase):\n"
                "    def test_a(self):\n"
                "        self.assertTrue(True)\n"
                "    def test_b(self):\n"
                "        self.assertTrue(True)\n"
            )
        with open(os.path.join(root, "test_bad.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(
                "import unittest\n"
                "class TestBad(unittest.TestCase):\n"
                "    def test_c(self):\n"
                "        self.fail('ожидаемо падает')\n"
            )
        res, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.collect_test_results, root)
        self.assertEqual(res["tool"], "unittest")
        self.assertEqual(res["failed"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(res["passed"], 2)
        self.assertFalse(res["ok"])


class TestRunParseSteps(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_marks_missing_scripts_and_data_as_skipped(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            steps, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_parse_steps, root)
            self.assertEqual(len(steps), len(orchestrator.PARSE_STEPS))
            self.assertTrue(all(s["skipped"] for s in steps))
            reasons = " | ".join(s["reason"] for s in steps)
            self.assertIn("listings.json", reasons)

    def test_json_to_db_skipped_without_data(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as root:
            steps, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.run_parse_steps, root)
            self.assertEqual(len(steps), len(orchestrator.PARSE_STEPS))
            self.assertTrue(all(s["skipped"] for s in steps))
            reasons = " | ".join(s["reason"] for s in steps)
            self.assertIn("listings.json", reasons)


class TestFindIssues(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_flags_failed_parse_step(self):
        step = {"name": "parser_cian.py", "skipped": False, "ok": False,
                "returncode": 2, "timed_out": False, "reason": ""}
        issues, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.find_issues, tempfile.mkdtemp(),
                            {"tool": "unittest", "ok": True, "passed": 3, "failed": 0, "errors": 0},
                            [step])
        self.assertTrue(any("parser_cian.py" in i for i in issues))

    def test_flags_failed_tests(self):
        test_res = {"tool": "unittest", "ok": False, "passed": 1, "failed": 2, "errors": 0}
        issues, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.find_issues, tempfile.mkdtemp(), test_res, [])
        self.assertTrue(any("2" in i for i in issues))

    def test_flags_token_named_file_in_root(self):
        root = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        with open(os.path.join(root, "токены.txt"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("x")
        issues, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.find_issues, root,
                            {"tool": "unittest", "ok": True, "passed": 1, "failed": 0, "errors": 0}, [])
        self.assertTrue(any("токены.txt" in i for i in issues))


class TestReport(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_build_report_contains_all_sections(self):
        root = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        with open(os.path.join(root, "parser_cian.py"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("# скрипт\n")
        test_res = {"tool": "unittest", "ok": True, "passed": 3, "failed": 0, "errors": 0}
        step = {"name": "check_cities.py", "skipped": True, "reason": "файл не найден",
                "ok": False, "returncode": None, "timed_out": False}
        issues = ["тестовая проблема"]
        text, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.build_report, root, test_res, [step], issues)
        for section in ("СТРУКТУРА", "ТЕСТЫ", "РАЗБОР ДАННЫХ", "ПРОБЛЕМЫ", "parser_cian.py", "тестовая проблема"):
            self.assertIn(section, text)

    def test_report_mode_writes_file(self):
        root = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        rc, _ = run_raw(orchestrator.report_mode, root)
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        path = os.path.join(root, "project_report.txt")
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(path))
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            content = f.read()
        self.assertIn("ПРОБЛЕМЫ", content)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Сторож капчи Яндекса. Запуск: python -m unittest test_parser_yandex"""
import os
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import parser_yandex


class FakeResp:
    def __init__(self, headers=None, text=""):
        self.headers = headers or {}
        self.status_code = 200
        self.text = text


def fixture(name):
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, "data", name), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()


class TestCaptchaGuard(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_header_captcha_detected(self):
        self.assertTrue(parser_yandex.captcha_hit(
            FakeResp(headers={"X-Yandex-Captcha": "captcha"})))

    def test_header_captcha_absent_ok(self):
        self.assertFalse(parser_yandex.captcha_hit(
            FakeResp(headers={"X-Yandex-Captcha": ""})))

    def test_robot_title_detected(self):
        self.assertTrue(parser_yandex.captcha_hit(
            FakeResp(text="<title>Вы не робот?</title>")))

    def test_real_catalog_page_ok(self):
        self.assertFalse(parser_yandex.captcha_hit(
            FakeResp(headers={"Content-Type": "text/html"},
                     text=fixture("yandex_page.html"))))

    def test_saved_404_page_is_not_captcha(self):
        self.assertFalse(parser_yandex.captcha_hit(
            FakeResp(text=fixture("yandex_404.html"))))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_setup_script.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""setup.ps1: источники токенов и защита от печати значений.
Запуск: python -m unittest test_setup_script"""
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SCRIPT = os.path.join(ROOT, "setup.ps1")


def run_setup(env, stdin_text=None, token_file=None):
    args = ["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass",
            "-File", SCRIPT, "-SkipGitHub"]
    if token_file:
        args += ["-TokenFile", token_file]
    return subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, input=stdin_text,
                          text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
                          env=env, cwd=ROOT, timeout=120)


class TestSetupScript(unittest.TestCase):
    def base_env(self):
        env = dict(os.environ)
        env.pop("BOT_TOKEN", None)
        env.pop("BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS", None)
        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
        env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"
        return env

    def test_from_environment_source_named(self):
        env = self.base_env()
        env["BOT_TOKEN"] = "111AAA:secretvalue"
        env["BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS"] = "222BBB:secretvalue"
        tmp = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "tok_setup_env.txt")
        r = run_setup(env, token_file=tmp)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
        self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN: заполнен (источник: окружение)", r.stdout)
        self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS: заполнен (источник: окружение)", r.stdout)

    def test_values_never_printed(self):
        env = self.base_env()
        env["BOT_TOKEN"] = "111AAA:supersecret111"
        env["BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS"] = "222BBB:supersecret222"
        tmp = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "tok_setup_env.txt")
        r = run_setup(env, token_file=tmp)
        self.assertNotIn("supersecret111", r.stdout)
        self.assertNotIn("supersecret222", r.stdout)
        self.assertNotIn("supersecret111", r.stderr)
        self.assertNotIn("supersecret222", r.stderr)

    def test_from_token_file(self):
        env = self.base_env()
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".txt")
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("# комментарий\nBOT_TOKEN=333CCC:filesecret\n")
        try:
            r = run_setup(env, token_file=path)
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
            self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN: заполнен (источник: токены.txt)", r.stdout)
            self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS: не задан", r.stdout)
            self.assertNotIn("filesecret", r.stdout)
        finally:
            os.remove(path)

    def test_manual_prompt_success_moves_to_file(self):
        env = self.base_env()
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".txt")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            r = run_setup(env, stdin_text="444DDD:typedsecret\n555EEE:typedsecret2\n",
                          token_file=path)
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
            self.assertIn("источник: ручной ввод", r.stdout)
            self.assertNotIn("typedsecret", r.stdout)
            content = open(path, encoding="utf-8").read()
            self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN=444DDD:typedsecret", content)
            self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS=555EEE:typedsecret2", content)
        finally:
            os.remove(path)

    def test_missing_report(self):
        env = self.base_env()
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".txt")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            r = run_setup(env, stdin_text="\n", token_file=path)
            self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)
            self.assertIn("не задан", r.stdout)
            self.assertIn("нет ни локально, ни в GitHub Secrets", r.stdout)
        finally:
            os.remove(path)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_token_env.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Токены только из окружения. Запуск: python -m unittest test_token_env"""
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
TG_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"\b\d{9,10}:A[0-9A-Za-z_-]{30,}")


def read(name):
    with open(os.path.join(ROOT, name), encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()


class TestBotReadsEnv(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_bot_tokens_come_from_env_var(self):
        self.assertIn('os.getenv("BOT_TOKEN")', read("bot.py"))

    def test_bot_does_not_reference_token_file(self):
        self.assertNotIn("токены", read("bot.py"))

    def test_bot_exits_with_clear_message_without_token(self):
        env = dict(os.environ)
        env.pop("BOT_TOKEN", None)
        env["PYTHONUTF8"] = "1"
        proc = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "bot.py"], cwd=ROOT, env=env,
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)
        self.assertNotEqual(proc.returncode, 0)
        self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN", (proc.stdout + proc.stderr))


class TestNoSecretsInScripts(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_bot_sources_have_no_hardcoded_tokens(self):
        for name in ("bot.py", "parser_cian.py", "parser_yandex.py"):
            self.assertIsNone(TG_TOKEN_RE.search(read(name)), name)

    def test_start_bats_have_no_hardcoded_tokens(self):
        for name in ("start.bat", "start_owners.bat", "start_realtor.bat",
                     "start_scheduler.bat"):
            if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, name)):
                continue
            self.assertIsNone(TG_TOKEN_RE.search(read(name)), name)

    def test_gitignore_covers_token_file(self):
        proc = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "check-ignore", "токены.txt"], cwd=ROOT,
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
        self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, ".gitignore не покрывает токены.txt")

    def test_workflows_pull_bot_tokens_from_github_secrets(self):
        text = read(os.path.join(".github", "workflows", "daily-parse.yml"))
        self.assertIn("${{ secrets.BOT_TOKEN }}", text)
        self.assertIsNone(TG_TOKEN_RE.search(text))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_warm_leads.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Тёплые клиенты: согласие, карточка лида, дубли, обработан. Запуск:
python -m unittest test_warm_leads"""
import datetime
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import warm_leads


def msk(y=2026, m=11, d=6, hh=14, mm=30):
    tz = datetime.timezone(datetime.timedelta(hours=3))
    return datetime.datetime(y, m, d, hh, mm, tzinfo=tz)


class WarmLeadsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.db = os.path.join(self.tmp.name, "warm_leads.db")
        self.log = os.path.join(self.tmp.name, "leads_log.txt")
        self.lead = {
            "rooms": "2", "area": 54, "floor": "5/9", "city": "Одинцово",
            "price_wish": "7,2 млн", "deadline": "ближайшие 1–3 месяца",
            "username": "andrey", "now": msk(),
        }

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def test_save_then_card_matches_template(self):
        _, row = warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        self.assertIsInstance(row, int)
        row, _ = warm_leads.get_lead(101, db_path=self.db)
        card = warm_leads.build_card(row, ref=self.lead["now"])
        self.assertEqual(card,
            "Собственник: согласен на звонок риэлтора\n"
            "Объект: 2-к, 54 м², 5/9, Одинцово\n"
            "Ожидаемая цена: 7,2 млн\n"
            "Срок: продам в ближайшие 1–3 месяца\n"
            "Готовность: сам нажал «нужна помощь» сегодня в 14:30")

    def test_dup_protection(self):
        warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        res, _ = warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        self.assertEqual(res, "exists")

    def test_mark_handled_and_new_lead_allowed(self):
        warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        self.assertTrue(warm_leads.mark_handled(101, db_path=self.db))
        res, _ = warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        self.assertEqual(res, "new")

    def test_list_and_mark_by_id(self):
        warm_leads.save_lead(201, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        warm_leads.save_lead(202, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        rows = warm_leads.list_leads(db_path=self.db)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 2)
        self.assertTrue(warm_leads.mark_handled_id(rows[0][0], db_path=self.db))
        self.assertEqual(len(warm_leads.list_leads(db_path=self.db)), 1)

    def test_deliver_writes_log_and_calls_sender(self):
        warm_leads.save_lead(101, self.lead, db_path=self.db)
        _, card = warm_leads.get_lead(101, db_path=self.db)
        sent = []
        warm_leads.deliver(card, log_path=self.log,
                           send=sent.append)
        self.assertIn("5/9, Одинцово", open(self.log, encoding="utf-8").read())
        self.assertEqual(card, sent[0])

    def test_card_skips_missing_optional_fields(self):
        lead = {"rooms": "2", "city": "Одинцово", "username": "a",
                "deadline": "ближайшие 1–3 месяца", "now": msk()}
        warm_leads.save_lead(102, lead, db_path=self.db)
        _, card = warm_leads.get_lead(102, db_path=self.db)
        self.assertNotIn("м²", card)
        self.assertNotIn("Ожидаемая цена", card)
        self.assertIn("Объект: 2-к, Одинцово", card)

    def test_yesterday_wording(self):
        lead = dict(self.lead, now=msk(m=11, d=5))
        warm_leads.save_lead(103, lead, db_path=self.db)
        row, _ = warm_leads.get_lead(103, db_path=self.db)
        card = warm_leads.build_card(row, ref=msk(m=11, d=6))
        self.assertIn("вчера в 14:30", card)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== test_wave3_speed.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Тесты Скоростного контура Волны 3 (A2/A4/A5 + миграция + сбор города).
Запуск: python -m unittest test_wave3_speed"""
import os
import sqlite3
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

import db_schema
import metrics
import parser_cian
import runner
import watchdog_db

MSK = timezone(timedelta(hours=3))


class TestNightWindow(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_boundaries_23_and_07(self):
        self.assertTrue(runner.is_night(datetime(2026, 11, 7, 23, 0, tzinfo=MSK)))
        self.assertTrue(runner.is_night(datetime(2026, 11, 7, 6, 59, tzinfo=MSK)))
        self.assertFalse(runner.is_night(datetime(2026, 11, 7, 7, 0, tzinfo=MSK)))
        self.assertFalse(runner.is_night(datetime(2026, 11, 7, 22, 59, tzinfo=MSK)))


class TestSchedule(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_first_due_immediately(self):
        self.assertTrue(runner.city_due("Одинцово", None, 1000.0, 3))

    def test_due_after_interval_with_zero_jitter(self):
        now = 10_000.0
        self.assertFalse(runner.city_due("X", now - 10, now, 3, jitter=0.0))
        self.assertTrue(runner.city_due("X", now - 3 * 60, now, 3, jitter=0.0))
        self.assertTrue(runner.city_due("X", now - 4 * 60, now, 3, jitter=0.0))

    def test_pick_due_checks_each_city(self):
        now = 1000.0
        last_runs = {"Одинцово": now - 10, "Химки": now - 4000}
        due = runner.pick_due(["Одинцово", "Химки"], last_runs, now, 3,
                              jitter=0.0)
        self.assertEqual(due, ["Химки"])


class TestConvergencePlan(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_cycle_plan_day_uses_groups(self):
        cfg = {"hot": {"cities": ["Одинцово"], "interval_minutes": 3},
               "regular": {"cities": ["Химки"], "interval_minutes": 17},
               "moscow": {"cities": ["Москва"], "interval_minutes": 15},
               "night": {"every_minutes": 60}}
        now = datetime(2026, 11, 7, 12, 0, tzinfo=MSK)
        plan = runner.cycle_plan(cfg, now, jitter=0.0)
        self.assertEqual(plan["kind"], "day")
        self.assertEqual(plan["hot"], ["Одинцово"])
        self.assertEqual(plan["regular"] + plan["moscow"], ["Химки", "Москва"])
        self.assertEqual(plan["intervals"]["hot"], 180.0)

    def test_cycle_plan_night_is_full(self):
        cfg = {"hot": {"cities": ["Одинцово"], "interval_minutes": 3},
               "regular": {"cities": ["Химки"], "interval_minutes": 17},
               "moscow": {"cities": ["Москва"], "interval_minutes": 15},
               "night": {"every_minutes": 60}}
        now = datetime(2026, 11, 7, 23, 30, tzinfo=MSK)
        plan = runner.cycle_plan(cfg, now, jitter=0.0)
        self.assertEqual(plan["kind"], "night")
        self.assertEqual(plan["all"], ["Одинцово", "Москва", "Химки"])
        self.assertEqual(plan["interval"], 3600.0)


class TestMoneyMetrics(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_to_utc_both_formats(self):
        self.assertIsNotNone(metrics.to_utc("2026-11-06T07:59:19.453464+00:00"))
        self.assertIsNotNone(metrics.to_utc("2026-11-05 10:13:49"))

    def test_summarize_avg_p95(self):
        vals = list(range(1, 21))  # p95: nearest-rank, 19-е значение из 20
        s = metrics.summarize(vals)
        self.assertEqual(s["n"], 20)
        self.assertEqual(s["avg"], 10.5)
        self.assertEqual(s["p95"], 19)

    def test_summarize_skips_none_and_empty(self):
        self.assertEqual(metrics.summarize([None, None])["n"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(metrics.summarize([])["p95"], None)

    def test_collect_from_db(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = sqlite3.connect(path)
            con.execute("CREATE TABLE listings (published_at TEXT, "
                        "detected_at TEXT, sent_at TEXT)")
            con.executemany(
                "INSERT INTO listings VALUES (?,?,?)", [
                    ("2026-11-07T10:00:00+00:00", "2026-11-07T10:05:00+00:00",
                     "2026-11-07T10:06:00+00:00"),
                    ("2026-11-07T11:00:00+00:00", "2026-11-07T11:20:00+00:00",
                     None)])
            con.commit()
            pub, sent = metrics.collect(con)
            self.assertEqual(pub, [300.0, 1200.0])
            self.assertEqual(sent, [60.0])
            con.close()
        finally:
            os.remove(path)


class TestSchemaMigration(unittest.TestCase):
    def _make_base(self, path):
        con = sqlite3.connect(path)
        con.execute(
            "CREATE TABLE listings (url TEXT PRIMARY KEY, city TEXT, "
            "price INTEGER, is_owner INTEGER)")
        return con

    def test_creates_table_on_fresh_db(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = sqlite3.connect(path)
            db_schema.ensure(con)  # пустая БД — таблица создаётся с нуля
            cols = [r[1] for r in con.execute("PRAGMA table_info(listings)")]
            for name in ("url", "city", "price", "is_owner", "source",
                         "region", "last_seen") + db_schema.LISTINGS_METRICS:
                self.assertIn(name, cols)
            con.close()
        finally:
            os.remove(path)

    def test_metrics_columns_and_queue_idempotent(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = self._make_base(path)
            db_schema.ensure(con)
            db_schema.ensure(con)  # дважды — идемпотентно
            cols = [r[1] for r in con.execute("PRAGMA table_info(listings)")]
            for name in db_schema.LISTINGS_METRICS:
                self.assertIn(name, cols)
            r = con.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode").fetchone()[0]
            self.assertEqual(r.lower(), "wal")
            pend = db_schema.pending(con)
            self.assertEqual(pend, [])
            con.close()
        finally:
            os.remove(path)

    def test_queue_notice_dedup_and_mark(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = self._make_base(path)
            db_schema.ensure(con)
            first = db_schema.queue_notice(con, "u1", "2-комн", "12", "t1")
            again = db_schema.queue_notice(con, "u1", "2-комн", "12", "t2")
            self.assertTrue(first)
            self.assertFalse(again)  # дубль не пишется
            other = db_schema.queue_notice(con, "u2", "1-комн", "12", "t1")
            self.assertTrue(other)
            self.assertEqual(len(db_schema.pending(con)), 2)
            db_schema.mark_delivered(con, [1], "t1")
            ids = [p[0] for p in db_schema.pending(con)]
            self.assertEqual(ids, [2])
            con.close()
        finally:
            os.remove(path)


class TestWatchdog(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_fresh_db_returns_zero_age(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = sqlite3.connect(path)
            con.execute("CREATE TABLE listings (last_seen TEXT)")
            con.execute("INSERT INTO listings VALUES (?)",
                        (datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),))
            con.commit(); con.close()
            minutes = watchdog_db.freshness_minutes(path)
            self.assertLess(minutes, 40)
        finally:
            os.remove(path)

    def test_stale_db_detected(self):
        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db")
        os.close(fd)
        try:
            con = sqlite3.connect(path)
            old = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=90)
            con.execute("CREATE TABLE listings (last_seen TEXT)")
            con.execute("INSERT INTO listings VALUES (?)",
                        (old.isoformat(),))
            con.commit(); con.close()
            minutes = watchdog_db.freshness_minutes(path)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(minutes, 40)
        finally:
            os.remove(path)


class FakeCianResp:

        self.text = text


class TestCollectCityHtmlOffline(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_merge_into_shared_state(self):
        def fake_fetch(sess, slug, page, label):
            assert slug == "odintsovo"
            html = ('/sale/flat/111/ ... Собственник ... /sale/flat/222/')
            return (["111", "222"] and
                    [{"id": str(i), "raw": {}} for i in ("111", "222")],
                    {"111"})
        listings, owners, seen = {}, {}, set()
        parser_cian.collect_city_html(
            None, "Одинцово", listings, owners, seen, log=lambda *_: None,
            fetch=fake_fetch)
        self.assertEqual(list(listings), ["111", "222"])
        self.assertEqual(listings["111"]["city"], "Одинцово")
        self.assertIn("111", owners)
        self.assertNotIn("222", owners)

    def test_unavailable_city_skipped(self):
        def fake_fetch(sess, slug, page, label):
            return None, set()
        listings, owners, seen = {}, {}, set()
        parser_cian.collect_city_html(
            None, "Нет-города", listings, owners, seen,
            log=lambda *_: None, fetch=fake_fetch)
        self.assertEqual((listings, owners), ({}, {}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Workflow daily-parse.yml: должен жить в .github/workflows с env из Secrets.
Запуск: python -m unittest test_workflow_location"""
import os
import unittest

import yaml

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
WF = os.path.join(ROOT, ".github", "workflows", "daily-parse.yml")


class TestWorkflowLocation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_file_in_github_workflows(self):
        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(WF), "workflow должен лежать в .github/workflows/")

    def test_valid_yaml_with_name(self):
        doc = yaml.safe_load(open(WF, encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(doc["name"], "daily-parse")

    def test_job_env_from_secrets(self):
        doc = yaml.safe_load(open(WF, encoding="utf-8"))
        job = doc["jobs"]["parse"]
        self.assertEqual(job["env"]["BOT_TOKEN"], "${{ secrets.BOT_TOKEN }}")
        self.assertEqual(job["env"]["BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS"], "${{ secrets.BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS }}")

    def test_secrets_check_step(self):
        doc = yaml.safe_load(open(WF, encoding="utf-8"))
        steps = doc["jobs"]["parse"]["steps"]
        names = [s.get("name") or s.get("uses") or "?" for s in steps]
        check = next(s for s in steps if s.get("name") == "Проверка секретов")
        self.assertIn("BOT_TOKEN_OWNERS", check["run"])

    def test_dispatch_and_cron(self):
        doc = yaml.safe_load(open(WF, encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertIn("workflow_dispatch", doc[True])  # ключ True: `on:` в yaml
        self.assertIn("cron", doc[True]["schedule"][0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


=== warm_leads.py ===
PROISHOZHDENIE: РЕПО-ИСТОРИЯ: коммиты Andrey/realtor-runner;первовиток вне git — НЕ ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНО
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Тёплые клиенты v1: согласие собственника на звонок риэлтора, карточка лида.

Хранилище: SQLite (WARM_LEADS_DB, по умолчанию warm_leads.db).
Дубли: у пользователя может быть ровно один необработанный лид.
Доставка: лог-файл + (при наличии) отправка прямо из бота (send-колбэк).
Время нажатия — МСК (UTC+3, летнего перевода нет).
"""
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import textwrap
import datetime

DB_PATH = os.getenv("WARM_LEADS_DB", "warm_leads.db")
LOG_PATH = os.getenv("WARM_LEADS_LOG", os.path.join("data", "warm_leads_log.txt"))
MSK = datetime.timezone(datetime.timedelta(hours=3))

_SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS leads (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    username TEXT,
    city TEXT,
    rooms TEXT,
    area REAL,
    floor TEXT,
    price_wish TEXT,
    deadline TEXT,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'new',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
)
"""


def _conn(path):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    conn.execute(_SCHEMA)
    return conn


def save_lead(user_id, payload, db_path=None):
    path = db_path or DB_PATH
    now = payload.get("now") or datetime.datetime.now(MSK)
    created_at = now.isoformat(timespec="minutes")
    username = payload.get("username")
    city = payload.get("city")
    rooms = payload.get("rooms")
    area = payload.get("area")
    floor = payload.get("floor")
    price_wish = payload.get("price_wish")
    deadline = payload.get("deadline")
    conn = _conn(path)
    try:
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, status FROM leads WHERE user_id=? AND status='new'",
            (user_id,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            return "exists", row[0]
        cur = conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO leads (user_id, username, city, rooms, area, floor, "
            "price_wish, deadline, status, created_at) "
            "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,'new',?)",
            (user_id, username, city, rooms, area, floor, price_wish,
             deadline, created_at))
        conn.commit()
        return "new", cur.lastrowid
    finally:
        conn.close()


def get_lead(user_id, db_path=None):
    path = db_path or DB_PATH
    conn = _conn(path)
    try:
        row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, username, city, rooms, area, floor, price_wish, "
            "deadline, status, created_at FROM leads "
            "WHERE user_id=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1", (user_id,)).fetchone()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    if not row:
        return None, ""
    return row, build_card(row)


def build_card(row, ref=None):
    """row: (id, username, city, rooms, area, floor, price_wish, deadline,
             status, created_at-iso).
    ref: текущее время МСК (по умолчанию — сейчас)."""
    (_id, _username, city, rooms, area, floor,
     price_wish, deadline, _status, created_at) = row
    ref = ref or datetime.datetime.now(MSK)
    created = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(created_at)
    parts = []
    if rooms:
        parts.append("%s-к" % rooms)
    if area:
        parts.append("%g м²" % area)
    if floor:
        parts.append(floor)
    if city:
        parts.append(city)
    obj = ", ".join(parts)
    lines = ["Собственник: согласен на звонок риэлтора"]
    if obj:
        lines.append("Объект: %s" % obj)
    if price_wish:
        lines.append("Ожидаемая цена: %s" % price_wish)
    if deadline:
        lines.append("Срок: продам в %s" % deadline)
    hhmm = created.strftime("%H:%M")
    if created.date() == ref.date():
        mark = "сегодня"
    elif (ref.date() - created.date()).days == 1:
        mark = "вчера"
    else:
        mark = created.strftime("%d.%m")
    lines.append("Готовность: сам нажал «нужна помощь» %s в %s" % (mark, hhmm))
    return "\n".join(lines)


def mark_handled(user_id, db_path=None):
    return _mark("user_id", user_id, db_path)


def mark_handled_id(lead_id, db_path=None):
    return _mark("id", lead_id, db_path)


def _mark(field, value, db_path):
    path = db_path or DB_PATH
    conn = _conn(path)
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            "UPDATE leads SET status='handled' WHERE %s=? AND status='new'"
            % field, (value,))
        conn.commit()
        return cur.rowcount > 0
    finally:
        conn.close()


def list_leads(status="new", limit=10, db_path=None):
    path = db_path or DB_PATH
    conn = _conn(path)
    try:
        return conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, user_id, username, city, rooms, area, floor, "
            "price_wish, deadline, status, created_at FROM leads "
            "WHERE status=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?", (status, limit)
        ).fetchall()
    finally:
        conn.close()


def deliver(card, log_path=None, send=None):
    log_path = log_path or LOG_PATH
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(log_path) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    with open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(datetime.datetime.now(MSK).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
                + " " + card.replace("\n", " | ") + "\n")
    if send:
        send(card)


=== watchdog_db.py ===

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""A3: проверка свежести БД для watchdog-воркфлоу.

Ручной запуск: python watchdog_db.py
Код возврата: 0 — БД отмечалась за последние FRESH_MINUTES минут,
2 — тишина дольше (в Actions запускается резервный прогон).
"""
import sqlite3
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import metrics

FRESH_MINUTES = 40
DB_PATH = "listings.db"


def freshness_minutes(path=DB_PATH):
    """Сколько минут назад БД «видела» объявления; None — данных нет."""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT last_seen FROM listings WHERE last_seen IS NOT NULL"
        ).fetchall()
    finally:
        conn.close()
    newest = None
    for (value,) in rows:
        dt = metrics.to_utc(value)
        if dt is None:
            continue
        if newest is None or dt > newest:
            newest = dt
    if newest is None:
        return None
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    return max(0.0, (now - newest).total_seconds() / 60.0)


def main():
    age = freshness_minutes()
    if age is None:
        print("freshness: данных о свежести нет")
        return 2
    status = "СВЕЖАЯ" if age <= FRESH_MINUTES else "ЧУЖАЯ-ТИШИНА"
    print("freshness: %s — %.1f мин назад" % (status, age))
    return 0 if age <= FRESH_MINUTES else 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
